                            - generic [ref=f1214e261]: Frenzy

                            - text: and

                            - text: to make

                            - strong [ref=f1214e282]: Fury Management

                          - generic [ref=f1214e703]:
                            - paragraph [ref=f1214e704]:
                              - generic [ref=f1214e705]: Aspect of Anger Management
                              - text: will be Transfigured on the Amulet. If you do not have the expansion put
                              - generic [ref=f1214e710]: Aspect of Anger Management
                              - text: on your pants.
                            - heading [level=4] [ref=f1214e715]:
                              - text: "Arsenal Selection Explaination: See"
                              - link "Arsenal Selection" [ref=f1214e716] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /diablo-4/builds/barbarian-frenzy-barb?ws-ngf5-1=activeVariantId%2Cd6a8ee9e-3f7f-4699-9fa7-120f08ff8824#arsenal-selection-1
                              - text: for this builds set up.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1214e717]:
                              - text: When choosing your
                              - strong [ref=f1214e718]: Barbarian
                              - strong [ref=f1214e719]
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e720]: Weapon Expertise Technique,
                              - text: you do not have to be using the weapon of the
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e721]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                              - text: you chose.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1214e722]: "Below is just an Example:"
                            - list [ref=f1214e723]:
                              - listitem [ref=f1214e724]:
                                - strong [ref=f1214e725]: Expertise
                                - text: chosen is
                                - strong [ref=f1214e726]: Polearm
                                - text: "- 15%(x) increased Lucky Hit Chance and you deal 15(x) increased damage while healthy"
                              - listitem [ref=f1214e727]:
                                - strong [ref=f1214e728]: Arsenal Selection
                                - text: ":"
                                - strong [ref=f1214e729]: Mace
                                - text: "- Lucky Hit: Chance to gain 5 Fury when hitting an enemy. Double the amount of Fury gained while Berserking and you deal 15(x) Critical Strike Damage while Berserking."
                            - paragraph [ref=f1214e730]:
                              - text: With the above set up, you gain the
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e731]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                              - strong [ref=f1214e732]
                              - text: bonus of having your
                              - strong [ref=f1214e733]: Arsenal Selection
                              - text: set to Two-Handed Mace, as well as the
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e734]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e735]
                              - text: bonus for the
                              - strong [ref=f1214e736]: Polearm Technique
                              - text: .
                            - paragraph [ref=f1214e737]:
                              - text: "To learn more about Arsenal Selections watch here:"
                              - link [ref=f1214e738] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ
                                - strong [ref=f1214e739]: Arsenal Selection Guide

                      - generic [ref=f1214e1067]:
                        - heading "Frequently Asked Questions" [level=2] [ref=f1214e1071]
                        - generic [ref=f1214e1078]:
                          - heading "Build Overview" [level=3] [ref=f1214e1079]
                          - heading "Arsenal Selection" [level=3] [ref=f1214e1080]
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1081]:
                            - text: This build will be running on a
                            - strong [ref=f1214e1082]: Dual Wield
                            - text: set up in order to take advantage of
                            - generic [ref=f1214e1083]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                            - text: combined with
                            - generic [ref=f1214e1088]: Shard of Verathiel
                            - text: .
                          - list [ref=f1214e1093]:
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1094]:
                              - strong [ref=f1214e1095]: "If you would like to learn how to set your weapon selection watch this video:"
                              - link "How to Select Weapons video" [ref=f1214e1096] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ?si=Lz-LY5MK3EehP_CC
                          - heading "Fury Management" [level=3] [ref=f1214e1097]
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1098]:
                            - generic [ref=f1214e1099]: Shard of Verathiel
                            - text: will cause
                            - generic [ref=f1214e1104]: Frenzy
                            - text: to cost
                            - strong [ref=f1214e1109]: Fury
                            - text: . That combined with
                            - generic [ref=f1214e1110]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                            - text: can be a strain on your fury. Below is your steps to manage that.
                          - heading "Affixes" [level=4] [ref=f1214e1115]
                          - list [ref=f1214e1116]:
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1117]:
                              - strong [ref=f1214e1118]: Resource Generation
                              - text: will help increase the amount of
                              - strong [ref=f1214e1119]: Fury
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1120]
                              - text: you gain and pairs really well with
                              - generic [ref=f1214e1121]: Rallying Cry
                              - text: . You can
                              - strong [ref=f1214e1126]: Temper
                              - text: this onto your
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1127]: Rings
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1128]: Amulet.
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1129]:
                              - strong [ref=f1214e1130]: Fury Regeneration
                              - text: needs to be added onto the Helmet, Chest piece, Pants and Boots.
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1131]:
                              - text: Do not spam
                              - generic [ref=f1214e1132]: Frenzy
                              - text: if you are not near monsters if you can't maintain
                              - strong [ref=f1214e1137]: Fury
                              - text: .
                          - heading "Defense (Non-Selig)" [level=4] [ref=f1214e1138]
                          - list [ref=f1214e1139]:
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1140]:
                              - text: Take all defense nodes inside the
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1141]: Paragon
                              - text: board first. All
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1142]: Maximum Life
                              - text: ","
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1143]: Resistances,
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1144]: Armor
                              - text: nodes.
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1145]:
                              - text: You can use glyphs like
                              - generic [ref=f1214e1146]: Ire
                              - text: or
                              - generic [ref=f1214e1151]: Territorial
                              - text: to help keep yourself alive.
                            - listitem [ref=f1214e1156]:
                              - text: Make sure all your gear has
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1157]: Maximum Life
                              - text: on it and you can then add
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1158]: Resistances,
                              - text: and
                              - emphasis [ref=f1214e1159]: Armor
                              - text: as needed.
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1160]:
                            - strong [ref=f1214e1161]: "If you want to learn how to set your defenses up, watch this video:"
                            - link "How to avoid getting one-shot video" [ref=f1214e1162] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/5IrPJ-H_uLI?si=yDaUSCn8owx0mDWj
                          - heading "Offense" [level=4] [ref=f1214e1163]
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1164]:
                            - strong [ref=f1214e1165]: "If you want to learn the basics about Multipliers watch this video:"
                            - link "Additive vs. Multiplicative Damage video" [ref=f1214e1166] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/xkaKBMKqPcs?si=kDEk0cc_up4pfUKj
                            - text: .
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1167]:
                            - strong [ref=f1214e1168]: Found a Mythic and want to know if it's useable?
                            - link "Is this Mythic usable with my build video?" [ref=f1214e1169] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/JGUtZ2TzXso?si=5cgPsJCZ1lJv-3rC
                          - heading "Early post 70 loot filter (All 850 items, in T1):" [level=4] [ref=f1214e1170]
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1171]: Cg8QAB0AAP//IgQIASAwKAEKDxAAHQAA//8iBAgDMAEoAQodEAAdAAD//yIECAEgeCIMCAUVgH4jABUF7SIAKAEKJhAAHQAA//8iGwgJFRX7IgAV5jEjABWTPCMAFYovIwAVQlkjACgBCiYQAB0AAP//IhsICRXp/CIAFUH7IgAVIFEjABXr/CIAFe78IgAoAQoPEAEdAAD//yIECAIgASgBEg9DbGlwdGlzIFN0YXJ0ZXIYASAB
                          - heading [level=4] [ref=f1214e1172]:
                            - strong [ref=f1214e1173]: "Mid game loot filter (Torment 4 to 8):"
                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1174]: ChEQAB0AAP//IgYIBCABMAEoAQodEAAdAAD//yIECAEgeCIMCAUVgH4jABUF7SIAKAEKJhAAHQAA//8iGwgJFRX7IgAV5jEjABWTPCMAFYovIwAVQlkjACgBCjsQAB0AAP//IgUIACiEByIECAMwASIbCAkV6fwiABVB+yIAFSBRIwAV6/wiABXu/CIAIgYIBCABMAEoAQoPEAMdAAD//yIECAEgDygBEg9DbGlwdGlzIE1pZGdhbWUYAiAC
                          - heading [level=4] [ref=f1214e1175]:

                          - paragraph [ref=f1214e1177]: ChoQAh0AAP//IgkIBhU4Xh0AIAEiBAgCIAQoAQoREAAdAAD//yIGCAQgATABKAEKHRAAHQAA//8iBAgBIHgiDAgFFYB+IwAVBe0iACgBCiYQAB0AAP//IhsICRUV+yIAFeYxIwAVkzwjABWKLyMAFUJZIwAoAQo7EAAdAAD//yIFCAAohAciBAgDMAEiGwgJFen8IgAVQfsiABUgUSMAFev8IgAV7vwiACIGCAQgATABKAEKDxADHQAA//8iBAgBIA8oARIPQ2xpcHRpcyBFbmRnYW1lGAMgAw==
